**THE GENERATION IS PART OF THE KEY (E58).** `clusters` `(generation, cluster_key)` · `cluster_members` `(generation, cluster_key, listing_id)` · `pairs` `(generation, listing_lo, listing_hi)`. Every writer writes and deletes strictly inside its own generation; every read joins on it. The one statement pair that crosses a generation is the score lane's optional prune (`keep_generations=<n>`, off by default), which drops whole old passes and never the one it just wrote. **Storage cost, measured live 2026-09-20 on the 4,569-listing trial cohort: ≈40 MB a generation** — `pairs` 91 MB over 39,832 rows across four passes (~2.3 kB a pair, features JSONB), so g5's 15,856 stored pairs are ~36 MB, plus ~1.7 MB of clusters and members (836 + 4,078 rows) and ~4 MB of `cluster_conflicts` (10,850 rows).

**`autodedup.verdicts` — what a ruling is about.** `generation` (nullable: NULL is legacy, not a claim) and `member_ids bigint[]` — the sorted listing ids the SERVER resolved for that `(generation, cluster_key)` at write time. A cluster ruling applies to a group only when `member_ids` equals its current member set; otherwise the group reads as unreviewed with a stale-verdict hint. Pair-grain rows carry neither, by CHECK.

**`autodedup.iterations` — the progress ledger the SPA renders** (§12). One row per iteration of any wave, written by the lane itself:

| column | type | meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | `bigserial` primary key | |
| `wave` | `text` | `W0`, `W0b`, `W1`, … |
| `title` | `text` | what this iteration tried, in a few words |
| `status` | `text not null check (status in ('running','done','failed','skipped'))` | |
| `approach` | `text` | the sentence the progress page shows |
| `tools` | `text[]` | the per-wave "Tools used" chips |
| `sample_stats` | `jsonb` | cohort, blocks, n pairs, n labels |
| `metrics` | `jsonb` | the numbers this iteration moved, each with its gate |
| `cost_usd` | `numeric(10,4) not null default 0` | **read from `llm_calls`** (E32), never forecast |
| `artifacts` | `jsonb` | name → url |
| `run_id` | `bigint` | the GitHub Actions run |
| `notes` | `text` | |
| `started_at` / `finished_at` / `created_at` | `timestamptz` | `created_at default now()` |

RLS on, `anon`/`authenticated` revoked, and registered — with every other new relation — in `tests/test_migration_rls_grants.py::_ADMIN_ONLY_RELATIONS`.

**Python package `autodedup/`:** `guards.py` (E2–E5, E34 — imports only `toolkit.room_taxonomy`) · `normalize.py` · `fingerprint.py` + `fingerprint_sql.py` · `blocking.py` + `blocking_sql.py` · `fetch.py` + `fetch_sql.py` · `features.py` · `model.py` · `decide.py` · `cluster.py` · `judge.py` + `judge_batch.py` · `apply.py` · `store.py` + `store_sql.py` · `iterations.py` (the ledger writer) · `evaluate.py` · `settings.py` · `census.py` **[built]** · `lane.py` **[built]**.

**CI and packaging chores:** `"autodedup"` in `tests/sql_corpus.py` `RUNTIME_DIRS` **[done]** so every `*_SQL` constant joins the PREPARE gate for free; `autodedup*` in `pyproject.toml` `packages.find` **[done]**; the new relations registered in `tests/test_migration_rls_grants.py::_ADMIN_ONLY_RELATIONS`; `frontend/public/workflow-docs.json` regenerated with `python scripts/generate_workflow_docs.py` whenever `autodedup.yml`'s leading comment changes (a **hard** CI gate); a `COPY autodedup/` line in the `Dockerfile` only if and when the worker lane ships.

**In-function concatenated SQL is invisible to the PREPARE gate.** Every executed statement lives in a module-level `*_SQL` constant with `%(name)s` params. Two grain variants mean two constants, never a string-built column name — the pattern `census.py` already follows.

## 14. Q13 — Wave plan, gates, cost

`ad_*` settings ship dark and **shadow mode is the only mode in W0–W7** (E39/D4). The program's terminal deliverable is **W6: a measured, fully evaluated engine that has written nothing**. Any canary or rollout after that is a separate operator decision (Appendix A).

| W | deliverable | gate | LLM cost |
|---|---|---|---|
| **W0** Census *(closed)* | `autodedup.yml` + `autodedup/lane.py` + `autodedup/census.py` on `main` — **PR #1482, merged as `f236d2f7`**; first census run **35088558149** | census artifact returned and readable — **met** (`autodedup-census-35088558149`, 17,455 B). The *delta* probes are W0b's gate, not W0's | **$0** |
| **W0b** Program doc + foundation *(this PR)* | `docs/design/autodedup/PROGRAM.md` + `README.md`; migrations **527** (`called_for`) and **528** (schema `autodedup`, incl. `iterations`) written **and applied**; the census **delta** probes: per-portal signal coverage (B1), remax location re-measure (B2), re-post base-rate (B3), developer-project density (B4), ingest-rate `L` (B5), RLS/grant state (B7), plus the block-layer adds `with_desc200` / `with_source_url` / `no_signal_at_all`. **Probe B6 (merge-ledger split) is dropped by D7** — the engine reads nothing from `property_merge_events`, not even counts | both migrations applied before merge; delta census runs green; **the operator picks the four blocks** off the table; `L`, the re-post base rate and `ad_max_cluster_size` are measured, not guessed | **$0** |
| **W1** Export + local harness + **progress page** | `mode=export` dumps the cohort as one gzipped JSONL artifact: fingerprints, **scrubbed** descriptions, phash lists, CLIP vectors as float16, tags, last 12 snapshots, **hashed** broker keys, location fields, and precomputed cross-evidence for every candidate pair — no PII in the artifact. Plus `GET /autodedup/iterations`, the `/autodedup/progress` SPA page and the `AUTODEDUP` nav entry, and `autodedup/iterations.py` so every lane from here on writes its own row | features for 10k pairs compute locally in < 60 s (zero-cost iteration proven); the progress page renders W0/W0b/W1 rows with real costs from `llm_calls` | **$0** |
| **W2** Blocking + core features | K1–K6, explosion control, catalog subtraction, the **core ~25 features** (grows only by error analysis), hand-initialised model; `estimate`/`verify` modes with a pure-Python oracle diffed against the SQL | oracle and SQL agree on every block, zero disagreements; ≤ 25 candidates/listing p50, ≤ 60 p99; certificates retrieve 100% of rule-certain positives; **`b` measured on the real cohort** | **$0** |
| **W3** Ground truth | vision-in-batch smoke (20); Qwen smoke (20); 4,000 cheap bootstrap labels; 1,200 gold three-vote labels; **measured output-token anchor** | gold unanimity ≥ 0.92; cheap-vs-gold ≥ 0.93; `llm_calls` shows the spend and the measured $/pair; every lane asserted "done" not "drew" | **$32** |
| **W4** Model + thresholds | Stdlib LR fit, reliability diagram, **per-stratum** `T_hi`/`T_lo` (D10), diversity-gate tuning, error analysis, up to 3 prompt iterations, feature-set growth driven by the named error classes | ECE ≤ 0.05; auto-band precision Wilson-LB ≥ 0.99 **on ≥ 380 pairs**, point ≥ 0.995, no stratum < 0.97; `b ≤ ad_band_budget`; weak strata explicitly demoted to propose-only | **$10** |
| **W5** Validation UI | `/autodedup`, `/autodedup/groups`, `/autodedup/residual`, `PairCard.tsx`, verdict capture, the "what would move" panel (E44/D5) | operator can scroll both views and verdicts persist; **the 200-pair gold-vs-operator session runs (D6) and reaches ≥ 0.95, or the program stops** | **$0** |
| **W6** Full trial pass — **the program's terminal gate** | Score all four blocks; judge the band; the recall probe; the exhaustive O(n²) bound on the sparse block; the sealed holdout; the decision package, including the reversibility argument **stated as argued, not measured** (A5) | every §9 criterion: precision per D3, cluster recall ≤ 1.15, missed-duplicate ≤ 2%, projected monthly ≤ $150. Outcome = a decision package for the operator, **not a rollout** | **$60** |
| **W7** Realtime lane — **design-only unless approved** | By default: a written lane design (this §11) plus the queue/enqueue/latch/plausibility-monitor specification, reviewed and left unbuilt. **Only on explicit operator approval** does it become code: worker lane, `resolve_queue`, enqueue hooks, latches, budget rails, `verify_pipeline` arm, E43 monitor, run 7 days with **writes off** | design-only: the operator has read it. If built: 7 days shadow, queue p95 < 60 s, zero lane crashes, measured spend within ±30% of forecast | **$0** design-only / **$10** if built |

**Committed development spend ≈ $102 design-only, ≈ $112 if W7 is built — of a $200 program cap (D2).** GPU $0 planned, **$20 reserved**, contingency **$68–78** — roughly two extra full W6 passes.

### Tools used, per wave (the progress page mirrors these as chips)

- **W0 — Census.** `autodedup/census.py` + `autodedup/lane.py` dispatched through `.github/workflows/autodedup.yml` (`mode=census`), read-only SQL over `listings` ⋈ `listing_location` ⋈ `location_granularity_rank`, `out/census.json` uploaded as a 30-day Actions artifact. No LLM, no GPU, no writes.
- **W0b — Doc + foundation.** `apply_migration.yml` (the no-MCP apply path) for 527/528; `psql "$SUPABASE_DB_URL"` for verification SELECTs; `tests/sql_corpus.py`'s PREPARE gate and `tests/test_migration_rls_grants.py` as the CI rails; the census lane again for the delta probes.
- **W1 — Export + harness + progress page.** `mode=export` on the same Actions lane (gzipped JSONL artifact, scrubbed and hashed); a local Python 3.12 stdlib harness reading that artifact; FastAPI (`api/routes/autodedup.py`) + React/Vite/Tailwind (`/autodedup/progress`, `Shell.tsx` nav); `autodedup/iterations.py` writing `autodedup.iterations`.
- **W2 — Blocking + core features.** Pure stdlib Python locally (no DB, no network): `blocking.py`, `features.py`, `guards.py`, plus the pure-Python oracle diffed against the SQL constants. `hashlib`/`unicodedata` for SimHash and deaccenting; `math` only. Zero cost, unlimited iterations.
- **W3 — Ground truth.** `LLMClient.call` via the OpenAI Batch API — `gpt-5.6-luna` (T1 text), `gpt-5-mini` (T2 vision, 768 px q80), a three-vote gold panel; every call lands an `llm_calls` row under `autodedup_judge_text` / `autodedup_judge_vision` / `autodedup_judge_gold`; `--max-usd` pre-flight asserting "done"; R2 presigned reads for the vision images.
- **W4 — Model + thresholds.** Stdlib logistic regression (`autodedup/model.py`), a 10-bin reliability diagram, Wilson and Clopper–Pearson intervals computed in `statistics`/`math`, Horvitz–Thompson weighting, bootstrap resampling — all local, all free. Prompt iterations go back through the W3 tools.
- **W5 — Validation UI.** FastAPI admin routes behind `require_admin`, React + TanStack Query on the SPA, `PairCard.tsx`, the shared `imageSrc` → `/images/{storage_path}` → 302 R2 media path, `lib/routes.ts` `def()` for every route literal.
- **W6 — Full trial pass.** Everything above at once: the Actions lane for the scored pass and the band judging, the local harness for evaluation (Wilson/Clopper–Pearson, Horvitz–Thompson, bootstrap), and `psql` for read-only verification SELECTs. `scripts/autodedup_revert.py` is *written and unit-tested against fixtures* here; it is not run against the database in this program (A5).
- **W7 — Realtime lane.** Design-only by default: this document plus a reviewed lane spec. If approved: `scraper/realtime_worker.py` lane tuple, `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` queue drain, lease-row CAS (`location_data/resolver/lease.py` pattern), `worker_heartbeats` + the Health page, `verify_pipeline` arm.

### Risks and mitigations

1. **Circular ground truth** — the judge is both arbiter and label source. → Three separately reported agreement numbers, model-family/vote/image-count separation between gold and cheap, the 200 operator-anchored pairs, and **gold-vs-operator < 0.95 stops the program** (D6). Residual risk: a blind spot shared by both OpenAI models and Qwen would survive all three checks.
2. **Developer projects** — the precision killer. → Five rails (§2), the dense Praha block, and the **assembled negative-control cohort** measured as its own stratum (D1). If precision there cannot clear 0.97, `project_suspected` blocks ship propose-only (D10).
3. **The band-width assumption drives every cost number.** → It is a *measured* quantity at the end of W2 and a settings-capped dial thereafter (E25), with the sacrificed recall reported. This is a mechanism, not a forecast.
4. **bazos may be undecidable at 99%** — zero area on 138,997 rows, no broker row, obec-grain pins, 57% pin/town disagreement. → Its own stratum and its own threshold (E23/D10); propose-only if it cannot clear. The area parser gap belongs to the scraper track.
5. **Vision-in-batch and Qwen are both unexercised in this repo.** → Smoke first (E31); fallbacks are sync vision at 2× and `gpt-5-mini` self-consistency n=3 (a weaker independence check, reported as such). The fallback is taken **per pair on any failure of the third arm, and the arm itself is switched off for the rest of the run** when the failure is permanent — an exhausted DashScope quota or a dead key, told apart from a burst 429 by `judge_lane.classify_provider_error` (quota / rate_limit / fatal / transient). A rate limit is retried at 2/4/8s first. Only an auth or config failure on the **primary** model, which has no second family behind it, stops a pass; `summary.json` publishes `qwen_arm_disabled`, its reason and the pair index it happened at, plus `weaker_votes`. Judge run 35146813906 is why: a quota 429 on the third arm stopped a gold pass after 598 of 2,240 calls while `gpt-5-mini` was answering every one of them.
6. **Reasoning tokens.** → Budgeted at 1,200–1,500 output tokens and replaced by the measured `llm_calls` average before any threshold is fixed (E32).
7. **The generation stamp is a non-atomic seam.** → Intent recorded before the call plus a reconciler sweep (E40). Not exercised in this program (D4), so the seam is a design obligation carried into Appendix A, not a live risk here.
8. **`MergeError` on a queue race.** → Explicit policy (E41): terminal vs retry vs poison, never silent.
9. **Guards at the wrong grain.** → Evaluated at property grain too (E4), or the engine manufactures merges the last rail would refuse and counts them as errors.
10. **Reversibility is asserted, not measured** — it is the claim that would license the threshold, and under D4 this program cannot measure it without doing the very writes it forbids. → It stays an *open* risk: W6's decision package states the claim as argued (from `unmerge_group`'s deterministic replay and the single-child group shape, E38) and names **A5 — the revert rehearsal** as the operator decision that must close it *before* any write path is switched on. Residual risk: `recompute_one` / `sync_browse_list` run inline per merge, so a bulk revert is a bulk read-model rebuild of unmeasured duration.
11. **Silent changes to operator state** (CLAUDE.md rules 16/18/22). → E44/D5: every proposal shows exactly what would move, in the UI, before anything is ever applied.
12. **Storage growth, and the temptation to index a hot shared table.** → Measured sizing, the `ad_anchor_images` knob, no multi-GB pair table, and **D8: no DDL on `public.images` — no shared-table index at all in this program**. If the image lane proves slow on the existing indexes, that is a finding for the rollout conversation, not a migration.
13. **Cron throttle** (80–256 minute gaps on a `*/30` lane). → Nothing latency-sensitive runs on cron; a future band drain would run sync inside the worker.
14. **Collision with the parallel operator-guided program.** → Separate schema, package, workflow, concurrency group, settings prefix, `called_for` values; **`llm_calls` is the only shared table this program writes** (three new `called_for` values), and under D7 this engine reads nothing the other program produces. Two engines eventually writing through one chokepoint is a **rollout** problem, and it is explicitly deferred to Appendix A rather than mitigated by importing the operator's merge history — D7 forbids that import.
15. **Every corpus number here is doc-sourced, dated 2026-09-08 to 2026-09-15, and unverified live.** W0's census is the first real measurement and may move the region choice, `L`, `c`, `b` and the cluster cap materially.

| M49 | what every judge arm — and every rule over their stored verdicts — is worth on band negatives drawn from MANY developments, and what each costs at M20's dial | D17's rates rested on 18 band negatives spanning 16 listings, 14 of them one operator card, and it ruled that only an operator pass could widen that axis | **the axis widened to 61 band negatives over 14 developments without an operator pass, and it splits the question in two: where the adverts PRINT a conflict every arm is competent, and where they print nothing that separates two units no arm is.** Four arms over M48's 248 structural band pairs (43 negatives / 11 blocks, 205 positives / 48 blocks) plus, as a second reference, W6's own 91 operator band labels (18 negatives / 5 blocks), **$2.7373 total**. On the STRUCTURAL negatives: `qwen3-vl-235b-a22b` strict **0/43, Wilson 95 % [0, 8.20], 0 blocks** ($0.003524/pair, p50 5.8 s) and `qwen3-vl-30b` strict **0/43** ($0.001732, p50 3.4 s), text `gpt-5.6-luna` j2 **3/43 = 6.98 %, 2 blocks** ($0.000758, p50 2.1 s), vision `gpt-5-mini` j2 **6/43 = 13.95 %, 5 blocks** ($0.004404, p50 14.1 s). **Every arm is 0/27 where the two bodies print conflicting unit numbers; every error any paid arm makes is on the 16 stated-area conflicts.** POOLED over both references (61 negatives / 14 blocks, 272 positives / 74 blocks): q235 **1/57 = 1.75 % [0.31, 9.29], 1 block**, recall 162/267; q30 **1/56**, recall 70/259; text **7/61 = 11.48 % [5.67, 21.84], 6 blocks**, recall 247/272; vision **17/61 = 27.87 %**, recall 254/272 — and the operator half is where the damage is (text 1/14 on `same_building_different_unit`, 2/2 on `same_project_different_unit`, vision 10/14). **All 18 operator band negatives print NO separating fact** — no unit designator on either side, no disjoint stated area, 17 of 18 inside one address block — and **no structural rule names a single one of them**, so the two negative sets are disjoint and the judge's residual job is precisely the case structure cannot reach. Of **162 rules scored free** over the stored verdicts (each arm alone, unanimity of 2/3/4, both cascade orders, a self-reported confidence floor, each under the all-arm abstain/same-building veto), the best safety per dollar is not an ensemble: **`text@0.95` — the text arm acting only on its own `same_property` at confidence ≥ 0.95 — makes 2/61 = 3.28 % [.., 11.19] over 2 blocks at 79.8 % band recall for text's own price**, beating `unan(text+vision)` and both cascade orders (4/61) at a fifth of the cost; `text@0.99` is 1/61 [.., 8.72] at 47.1 %. The floor is CHOSEN on the pairs that score it, so its interval is optimistic and it needs a confirmation pass. At M20's production dial (296,550 band pairs a month) nothing else is near the budget: text and every gate on it **$224**, q30 **$507**, `casc(text>q235)` **$1,014**, q235 **$1,030**, vision **$1,314**, any all-arm veto **$3,071**; at D15's 75 %-recall dial text is **$86** and remains the only measured configuration inside $50–150 | judge runs `35490419839` `35490587387` `35490794443` `35491042156` `35491642944` `35492031522` `35492862019`; `w7/judges/{findings,index,masking,confidence,proposal_precision,operator_negative_anatomy}.json` + `{structural,operator,pooled}/compare.json` | W7 |

No negative carries a shared code and **27 of 27 `neg_unit_number_conflict` pairs show a unit designator on BOTH sides**, which is the right exposure for a negative and a confound for a positive. | M150 | whether the live lane's FACTS are the batch engine's facts, field by field | M83 closed the listing half and nothing had ever compared the image half | **the listing half holds; the image half does not.** Over a 200-listing source-stratified sample of the g6 cohort read twice — once from export run `35200225251`, once through `SqlFacts` — every listing-field difference is genuine drift since the export (`last_seen_at` 78, `is_active`/`inactive_at` 4, `price`/`price_history` 4 and all four of those on listings the snapshot stream says changed). The image half: **`pop` differs on 2,832 of 2,847 images (99.5 %), artifact ≥ 1 against live 0**, because `autodedup.phash_pop` holds **0 rows** and nothing writes it; and 557 producer differences over five fields (`phash` 120, `clip` 212, `tags` 211, `clip_present` 6, `tags_len` 8) where the pHash and CLIP jobs re-ran after the export. Downstream: **`catalog_ratio_max` moved on 387 of 400 sampled pairs (96.8 %)**, `n_images_min` on 140 (mean 2.22 images), `pair.probes` on 17 — the retrieval keys themselves move, because `anchor_bands` are built from the non-catalogue gallery | `/home/hejtm/autodedup-artifacts/w9g/parity_before/`, lane runs `35522590316` and `35522827863` (`--mode rt_parity`) | W9g |
| M151 | which of the two live defects costs the certificates and which costs the scores | one live pass showed both at once — 0 K-C and 5,888 of 7,878 pairs moved — and the program had no way to attribute either | **they are separate and they do not overlap.** Holding settings, model and calibration equal on both sides and moving ONLY the facts: **every K-C in the sample is lost (3 of 3, `K-C->-`) while every K-B survives (3 of 3)** — K-C requires `catalog_ratio_max` to be PRESENT and K-B does not read it — and the score moves past 0.01 on just **5 of 400 pairs under `hand_v1` and 6 of 400 under `w6_gold`**, with **one** band→merge flip and none under `w6_gold`. So the missing certificates are the FACT defect and nothing else, and the 5,888 moved scores are not the facts: the generation was seeded and run on `Settings()` + `hand_initialised()` against g6's `w8` + `w6_gold` — `t_lo 0.30` vs `0.1823`, `t_hi 0.97` vs `1.0`, no `t_hi_by_stratum`, no bridge, no context rule (E90a) | the same two runs; `lane_config.settings_differences` in `parity.json` | W9g |
| M152 | what re-clustering costs, and whether the first pass's 682 s is a per-pass cost | unmeasured — E72 counted components and never priced one | **it is a FIRST-pass cost with a per-pass shape that can return.** The live pass spent **682.2 s of 876.7 s in `cluster_s` and ~7,131 statements over 700 components — 0.97 s and ~10 statements a component** at ~96 ms a round trip to Frankfurt, because `_recluster` is spelled per component: a BFS `merge_neighbours` per frontier level, then `pairs_within`, `clusters_touching`, a `working.ensure` fact read and up to six writes, each a serial round trip. It was 700 components because the store was EMPTY: every one of the 36,940 pairs scored fresh and all 4,558 merge-zone pairs seeded. A steady-state pass re-scores only pairs touching the claim (77 listings, 0.88 arrivals a pass measured) and seeds a handful. But the cost returns whole on any pass that touches the merge graph at scale — a rescope, a new generation, a large delist batch — so `_recluster` is the one loop of this engine still spelled per unit rather than per pass, which is exactly what E74 names | `/home/hejtm/autodedup-artifacts/w9f/pass_35520830343/` summary; `incremental._recluster` | W9g |
| M153 | what a re-cluster costs once it is spelled per PASS instead of per component | M152 priced the per-component shape at ~10 statements and 0.97 s a component | **constant in the component count.** Same store, same seeds, old code against new: **700 components cost 4,200 store calls + 700 fact reads (~7,700 statements, 736.9 s at the measured 96 ms round trip) before; 4 store calls + 1 fact read (9 statements, 0.9 s) after** — and 10 components cost the same 4 + 1 as 700. The before number reproduces the live pass's 7,131 statements and 682 s, which is what says the benchmark is measuring the right thing | `/home/hejtm/autodedup-artifacts/w9g/cluster_cost/cluster_cost.json`; `tests/autodedup/test_rt_gate.py::test_the_re_cluster_costs_the_same_statements_whatever_the_component_count` | W9g |

What remains is producer movement (557 over `phash`/`clip`/`tags`) and drift (`last_seen_at` 78, `is_active`/`inactive_at` 4, `price`/`price_history` 4 with `differ_stable` 0) — every difference classed, none unexplained. ### Decisions ledger

D1–D10 ruled on **2026-09-16**, **ruled by: program lead (operator delegated)**. These rulings are binding and supersede the design text wherever the two differ.

| # | decision | ruling | date |
|---|---|---|---|
| D1 | trial cohort shape and size | **Four blocks sized to budget:** primary district town 2,000–3,500 all-time; a sparse town 500–700 (which is also the exhaustive O(n²) blocking bound); one Praha `cast_obce_kod` quarter 1,000–1,500 (developer stress); an assembled negative-control set ≤ 800 (same `ruian_adm_kod` different floor + shared dHash on ≥ 5 listings). **Final pick after the census.** | 2026-09-16 |
| D2 | spend gate mechanics | **<$10/run autonomous; $25 hard cap per run; $200 total program.** Every paid lane carries `--max-usd` and asserts the batch read **"done"**, not merely "drew". | 2026-09-16 |
| D3 | the precision bar, as arithmetic | **Wilson lower bound ≥ 0.99 on n ≥ 380 auto-merge pairs, point ≥ 0.995, and no stratum below 0.97.** | 2026-09-16 |
| D4 | shadow vs canary | **The ENTIRE trial is shadow mode — decide, store, write nothing.** Any canary or rollout is a later operator decision and is explicitly *not* part of this program's deliverable (Appendix A). | 2026-09-16 |
| D5 | operator-state movement | **Adopted for the (future) write path: report what operator state moved** — and, in shadow, what *would* move, shown per proposal in the validation UI (E44). | 2026-09-16 |
| D6 | the circularity stop | **Adopted: gold-vs-operator ≥ 0.95 on a 200-pair operator session run through the validation UI, else the program stops.** The session's own tooling is **E55** (seeded random sample order, a progress counter, blind review) and the number is read live by `GET /autodedup/agreement` — binary comparison, explicit **and** cluster-implied operator labels, gold > vision > text, `insufficient_evidence` counted apart, Wilson 95% (§9). | 2026-09-16 |
| D7 | how clean is the clean slate | **TOTAL. The engine reads NOTHING from `property_merge_events` — no unmerge or split history, no seeds, no evaluation labels, not even counts. Census probe B6 is dropped.** The generation stamp survives only as a future *write* (E40). | 2026-09-16 |
| D8 | storage: anchor images and the shared-table index | **3 anchor images. NO shared-table index — no DDL on `public.images` — anywhere in this trial.** | 2026-09-16 |
| D9 | backfill budget | **Corpus backfill is out of scope for this program.** Its arithmetic is retained in Appendix A for the later conversation only. | 2026-09-16 |
| D10 | what the engine may give up | **Per-stratum thresholds; weak strata ship propose-only.** | 2026-09-16 |
| D11 | how W4 ships against D3 | **Ruled by the W4 decision-maker, 2026-09-16.** D3's two halves separate on this label set. (i) The **point** bar (≥0.995) and the **0.97 stratum floor** are the ship rule and are met: `K-B|same` 99.09 %, `K-C|cross` 100 %, `model|cross` 100 % at `T_hi` 0.98939; `K-C|same`, `model|same` and both `K-A` strata ship **propose-only** under E48. (ii) The **Wilson LB ≥ 0.99** bar is **not met and is not reachable**: with k = n the bound is n/(n+z²), so a flawless cell needs **381** judged merges and the entire judged merge set is 292 (the sealed split's is 22). It is therefore reported as **label-bound, not model-bound**, and the next move is ~400 more gold labels drawn *inside* the merge zone — not a further rule. The one residual false merge (a catalogue-only same-portal re-post, K-B) is left live **deliberately**: every clause that kills it is fitted to that single pair and costs 1.7–28 % of the merge set, and no score cut separates it (it sits at 0.7014 between true merges at 0.7016 and 0.6504). | 2026-09-16 |

| D12 | the OSS judge arm | **Ruled by the W4e decision-maker, 2026-09-16. Not usable at 7B.** Qwen2.5-VL-7B on a community RTX 3090 pod agrees with gold on **0.652** of binary pairs (κ 0.24) at $0.00032/pair and 11 s/pair, and its errors are not noise — it is biased toward `same_property`, which is the one direction a merge engine cannot absorb. The paid arms stay: gpt-5-mini vision (0.971 / κ 0.94) for the labelling lane, gpt-5.6-luna text (0.973 / κ 0.95) where its 22 % abstention is affordable. **32B AWQ is UNTESTED, for capacity, not for quality** — no A40/A6000 community capacity was available in any attempt, so the 32B arm is neither adopted nor refuted. | 2026-09-16 |
| D13 | plot contradiction as a hard reject (the proposed E49) | **Ruled by the W4e decision-maker, 2026-09-16. NOT shipped — the evidence refutes the premise.** The three "plot-contradiction false merges" (1700/700, 2239/239, 5870/870) are all **true duplicates with an identical description**, mis-read because the ceskereality side's plot is a thousands-truncation artefact (M24); the judge called them different on a corrupted field. With the truncating carriers gated, **0 of 42** judged duplicates carrying two trusted plots disagree by >30 %, and exactly **1 of 9** non-duplicates does — a terminal auto-reject resting on n=1, against a second confounder that is INFERRED, not labelled: one Turnov villa has two sreality rows, 85063 at estate 5,870 m² and 186106 at 3,230 m² — one quotes the parcel set, the other one parcel, and that pair carries no judge verdict. (The pair 186106 × 444982, which reads 3,230 vs 870, is not the confounder: 870 is the ceskereality truncation of 5,870 and the carrier is gated). The two features ship and the model may weigh them at the next refit; no guard, no settings knob, no dead code. | 2026-09-16 |
| D14 | room-tag-paired image evidence — the operator's kitchen-to-kitchen instruction | **Ruled 2026-09-17 by the W5 decision-maker. ADOPTED as features and as the judge's presentation; REFUSED as positive merge evidence; the refit is NOT promoted.** (i) The instruction is right and the first reading of it was wrong: the diagnosis measured the **maximum** tag-matched cosine (facade-dominated, AUC 0.543 in the hazard cell) where the discriminating statistic is the **weakest non-hallway room** — AUC **0.820** (bootstrap 95 % CI 0.697–0.911), **the only above-chance image signal that cell has** (M28, M33). (ii) It ships as **twelve append-only `FEATURE_ORDER` slots, `FEATURE_VERSION` 3 → 4 (47 → 59)**, learned rather than ruled: `tag_dhash_min` takes the 6th largest weight of 59 at the refit. (iii) It **may never act as positive, merge-supporting similarity**: a room-matched cosine reaches 0.977 between two different buildings of one project while a true duplicate's re-shot kitchen sits at 0.929 (M33). It pays DOWNWARD. (iv) **No new gate** — every candidate merge-zone gate costs judged positives and removes zero judged negatives (M31); the weights did the work instead. (v) **The refit is measured and NOT promoted**: no `settings/w5.json`, no `models/w5_gold.json`. W5-B is better where the operator is looking (merge recall 39.99 % → 41.53 %, the band 15.43 % → 12.41 % of scored, the operator's group 8 band pairs → 5, the true-vs-worst-false margin 0.369 → 0.487) and worse on every precision column, **all of it turning on ONE judged negative** — `257939 × 415376`, the 1700/700 warehouse **D13 has already ruled a true duplicate** mis-judged on a thousands-truncated plot field (M24, M30). Corrected, W5-B reads 451/453 = 99.56 % and its sealed cell 23/23. **Two gold votes on that one pair (≈ $0.01) decide whether it ships**, alongside the ~30 `model|same` merge labels `MIN_STRATUM_N` is already waiting for — and there is now a cheaper settlement than either: the scraper track shipped **one area-number grammar for every portal** while this wave ran (`3aad0755` / #1503, spaced thousands no longer truncate, 17k ceskereality + 13k realitymix rows re-parsed; `0ab92e4a` / #1507 for RE/MAX), so **M24's carrier is fixed at the source**. The cohort artifact `35096363646` predates it and has the 1700/700 reading frozen in, so a **re-export settles this pair for $0** and re-opens M24, M25 and D13 on healed data. Do that before spending on gold votes. Until then the generation stays **g3** and `w4f_gold` warns at load about the 12 slots it cannot score. **SUPERSEDED by D16 (2026-09-17): the re-export was done and it healed the pair, a 60-pair gold re-judge of the merge boundary showed the cheap labels understate merge precision asymmetrically, and the refit was promoted as generation g4.** | 2026-09-17 |
| D15 | the band judge at rollout | **Ruled 2026-09-17 by the W5 decision-maker. The paid two-tier judge stays: `gpt-5.6-luna` text on 100 % of band pairs + `gpt-5-mini` vision on the ~22 % it abstains on, under the `j2` presentation (E53), at $0.001724/pair = $383/month (trial c 7.99) or $720/month (production c 15.03) for 95 % recall, and $148 / $277 at 75 % recall (the W4f cost dial, M20).** The operator has **rejected the OpenAI Batch API**: rollout must decide in real time, and both paid tiers do (16–20 s/pair at 6–8 workers), so no option here depends on batching. **Every alternative was measured and every one is refused** (M34, M36): vision on 100 % of the band is $982 / $1,848 for the same recall; the self-hosted Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B is the cheapest thing in this program ($0.000533/pair, 7.5 s) and the safest arm measured (98.7 % same-property precision, zero false calls on a gold `different_property`) but finds **23 %** of duplicates, and escalating what it does not decide costs MORE than simply calling `gpt-5-mini` (it saves 0.7 %); the dense Qwen3-VL-32B is decisive (κ 0.543, 79.5 % recall) and merges two units of one building at **3× the paid rate for 11 % less money**, which is the one trade this engine's standing rule forbids; the DashScope-hosted 30B as the gold independence arm is **retired** — it moved 3 stored labels in 553 pairs (0.54 %) for $0.99 and two provider incidents. **The 30B's one sanctioned use is a logged corroborating signal on the merge zone, never a decider.** D12 is extended, not overturned: 7B is biased toward `same_property`, 30B-A3B against it, 32B dense toward the building. **`j2` ships as the presentation but is NOT yet the band decider**: it is measurably a different comparison (37 % of frames, 89 % of pairs get a room pair) and in the band it is exactly as safe as j1 (6 false `same_property` each), but its aggregate agreement is 1.7 points lower at p = 0.275 against gold that **contains two j1 votes**, so the metric cannot separate 'j2 is worse' from 'gold is wrong where j1 was wrong'. The settling move is **~14 gold-quality re-judges of the discordant pairs, or operator adjudication of them — five are pairs a j1 verdict would merge and should not** — plus the j2b prompt line both genuine j2 misses name: a room pair that merely LOOKS alike is not a match, and a stated unit-level conflict outranks photographic resemblance. | 2026-09-17 |
| D16 | whether the v4 refit ships | **Ruled 2026-09-17 by the W5d decision-maker. PROMOTED — `autodedup/settings/w5.json` + `w5_strata.json` + `autodedup/models/w5_gold.json`, generation `g4`.** D14 refused the same model and named the two things that would settle it; both were done, and together they invert the verdict. (i) **The re-export on healed data** (M37) removed the artefact D13 had already ruled on: 217 area fields moved, 78 % of them by an exact multiple of 1,000, and `257939 × 415376` now agrees at 1700 m² on both sides. (ii) **A 60-pair gold re-judge of the merge boundary** (M38, run `35205840437`, $0.621704) — every judged negative either arm auto-merges, plus 48 judged positives drawn at random from the candidate's own merge set, so both flip directions are measured. The cheap tier turns out to understate merge precision **58 % of the time one way against 2.1 % the other**, and two false merges this program has carried since W4 (`14093 × 149907`, `15034630 × 18854134`) were never false merges at all. (iii) **On the bar this wave was given the candidate meets every clause** (M39): pooled merge precision **100.00 % against the incumbent's 100.00 %**, with a Wilson lower bound of **99.24 % against 99.10 %** over a merge set 17 % larger (499 judged merges against 425); the sealed holdout at the settings in force is **111 merges, 100 %, min cell 100 %, floor ok**; the band FALLS from 45.35 % to **33.81 %** of stored pairs (15.25 % → **10.96 %** of scored, $402/$703 → **$289/$505** a month), and merge-zone recall rises **37.97 % → 47.15 %**. (iv) **`model|same` does NOT leave propose-only**, and the reason changed (M40): the count bar is met — 89 judged merges — but `28071 × 18661514` scores 1.000000 and is gold `different_property`, so no cut clears the 0.995 point gate. The variant that ships it at 0.9834 was measured (merge recall 54.12 %, band 32.43 %) and **refused**: it costs the one false merge and drops the pooled lower bound below the incumbent's, and the cut that clears the sealed floor sits 3 × 10⁻⁵ above a sealed negative — a threshold fitted to the holdout, which this program does not publish. `model|cross` ships at **0.9788**, the dev search's own point-gate answer, with the sealed split agreeing at 34/34 — no cut was raised to meet a holdout. (v) **One gate fails and is recorded rather than waived**: E21's ≤ 5 % sealed ECE reads **5.75 %** for `w5_gold` against the incumbent's 3.98 % (M41). The only map that passes is at chance in the merge end and has a ceiling below the shipped cut, so it cannot express the decision; the shipped map's merge-end ECE is 3.85 %. The cut is measured on labels, not read off the probability, so the failure does not reach the merge rule — but it is the one number a reader should hold against this promotion, and it is the same ask as D11's: more merge-end labels. (vi) **The fit corpus deliberately excludes run `35205840437`** — those pairs were selected by the candidate's own errors, and a fit on them would be adaptive; they are used for measurement only. | 2026-09-17 |
| D17 | the judge at rollout, re-measured against the operator | **Ruled 2026-09-20 by the W6 decision-maker. NO judge arm gets merge authority, and D15's escalation half is SUSPENDED.** The operator's own band labels are now the reference, and on them (M45) the ruled D15 rule — `gpt-5.6-luna` text on 100 % of the band, `gpt-5-mini` vision on the ~22 % it abstains on — makes **12 false merges of 18 band negatives (66.7 %)**, worse than vision alone, because the vision tier resolves the text tier's abstentions toward `same_property`. Text's apparent safety was an abstention artefact: it abstains on 10 of those 18 negatives, and abstain was scored as no-merge everywhere. **What ships as the recommendation is a FILTER, not a decider**: `gpt-5.6-luna` text under `j2` on 100 % of the band, `same_property` proposes, everything else — including every abstention — goes to the operator's residual queue and nothing auto-merges on a judge verdict. Cost at M20's production dial (296,550 band pairs a month): **$217/month at 95 % recall**, and at D15's own 75 %-recall dial ratio (0.386) **≈ $84/month**, which is the only measured configuration inside the operator's $50–150. Full-band vision is $1,229, the confirm-cascade $1,132, gold $3,091 — all refused on cost, and none of them is safe enough to be worth the money anyway (best measured false-merge rate on the band is 11.1 %, 95 % upper bound 42 %). **The open-weights arm is re-opened, not adopted**: DashScope is live again and `qwen3-vl-235b-a22b` is the best single arm on precision measured in this program (1/13 false merges, 3.2 % of merges wrong, $0.00334, 5.3 s — four times faster than `gpt-5-mini` at the same price) but finds only 51 % of duplicates, so it is a precision filter, never a finder; D12's 7B verdict stands. **No presentation change**: j2 stays the default `JUDGE_VERSION` — the j2-vs-j1 gap is 3 pairs (McNemar p ≈ 0.51) and it reverses on the common set, so nothing here separates them. **Two operational findings bind the next paid pass**: `judge_lane.classify_provider_error` maps DashScope's pacing 429 (which quotes billing wording) to `ERROR_QUOTA` and kills the arm — two runs were lost to it and `workers=1` completed cleanly — and the gold artifact double-books cost (223 aggregate rows carry the same $2.3245 as the 669 votes). **The number that limits every one of these rates is the label set, not the models**: 18 band negatives spanning 16 listings, 14 of them from one operator card, so no false-merge rate below ~20 % is demonstrable at 95 % confidence. The next operator pass should draw band NEGATIVES from other developments; more judge money cannot buy this. | 2026-09-20 |
| D18 | whether anything from W6 is promoted, and what g5 is | **Ruled 2026-09-20 by the W6 decision-maker. The REFIT is refused; a CLUSTERING-ONLY generation g5 is PROMOTED — `autodedup/settings/w6.json` + `w6_strata.json` + `autodedup/models/w6_gold.json`, whose weights are byte-identical to `w5_gold`'s.** (i) **No W6 model arm ships** (M43): at equal thresholds every arm is worse than the incumbent, the candidate's operating point is a 1,622-pair isotonic plateau switch 7 × 10⁻⁵ wide that admits five labelled negatives, its threshold search consumed the adaptive re-judge D16(vi) reserved for measurement, and its E21 'settlement' is a population change. Against operator truth it doubles the false merges, both in the same new development, against the standing ruling. | D33 | how the live lane obtains a corpus-wide statistic it may not recount | **THE SEED CARRIES IT, AND WHAT IT DOES NOT CARRY IS UNKNOWN, ruled 2026-09-20 by the W9g implementer on the catalog and the parity evidence; $0.** (i) `autodedup.phash_pop` is written by ONE writer, `rt_seed`, and what it writes is the ARTIFACT's own `pop` — the corpus-wide count the export already paid one sequential scan for. The live lane therefore never scans `public.images`, and it joins against exactly the statistic the batch engine scored with. (ii) There is no cheaper measurement for a hash the export never saw: `public.images` carries no index on `phash` (checked live; `images_phash_idx` is on `sreality_id`), ruling D8 forbids adding one, and no table in schema `autodedup` mirrors (hash → listing) — `image_band` is a per-listing dHash LSH and holds 0 rows. (iii) So a new hash reads **UNKNOWN, never 0**, and the staleness is stated rather than papered over: the population is as of the export, it can only have grown, an unmeasured photograph costs its listing the `catalog_ratio` (K-C cannot fire for it) and never claims the photo is unique, and `population.images_unmeasured` on every pass summary is the number that says when a fresh export and re-seed are owed. (iv) A per-pass top-up was considered and REJECTED: it is the same sequential scan, inside the pass's transaction, where a timeout would abort the pass — and it would un-freeze under a running calibration the one statistic E70 froze. | 2026-09-20 |
| D34 | whether a real-time merge may be taken before the listing's photographs have been processed | **NO — it is HELD, and the wait is bounded and named. Ruled 2026-09-20 by the W9h implementer on the arrival-timeline measurements; $0.** (i) ~80% of arrivals are claimed 5–15 min after `first_seen_at` while their `phash` is written hourly at :20 and their CLIP vector at :40 (p50 2.47 h, p90 4.51 h), 78% never get a second snapshot, and no feed was keyed on either arriving — so a blind decision was a FINAL decision. (ii) A merge that rests on photographs therefore waits in the BAND with reason `evidence_pending` while a side's gallery carries no hash at all and is inside `rt_evidence_horizon_hours` (48 h); a merge resting on K-B or K-R waits for nothing, because neither reads a photograph; a REJECT is never held, since it was taken on evidence the lane has. (iii) The wait is the producers' own distribution and it is stated rather than hidden (M119). (iv) The alternative — merging blind and un-merging later — is REFUSED: this program never unmerges, and a group formed on evidence that does not exist yet is the one thing shadow mode exists to prevent. (v) The alternative that would remove the wait entirely, hashing on download in `scraper/`'s image worker, is OUTSIDE this program's territory (D26, ruling D8's sibling) and is the operator's call; this lane may name it and must not reach for it. (vi) The hold is an ARGUMENT to `run_pass`, not a settings row, so a batch pass and the replay behave exactly as before and E70's equivalence proof stays exact — with a variant that withholds the evidence and still reaches the batch state (M118). | 2026-09-20 |
| D36 | what W13 promotes after its verification | **GENERATION g7: the honest live-window clock ships, priced by the E84 rail alone.** Ruled 2026-09-20 by the W13 assembler on the verifier's single sealed read of `510db099…` (seed 20260927), which this wave opened ONCE and has now recorded as SPENT; $0, one `mode=labels` lane run, no judge, no DB write, D19 intact. (i) **What changed is a LABEL, not a measurement.** The arm is W11's exactly — honest clock (E62) + the E84 one-minute pair gap, **no E65 image floor, no E83 carrier-aware stock, no E85 family guard, no E88 hold**, `w6_gold` unrefitted — and the one number that refused it was the sealed gold negative `522698 x 13221982`. **On 2026-09-20 19:30 UTC the operator ruled that pair and its dev-side twin `555448 x 18626270` `same`** in the validation UI (explicit, pair grain, `same_property`, `op:0871b32a9d227f60`; M125). Operator labels outrank gold, so the count went to 0 and E110 retires the demand the gate was making. The verification did not take the ruling on trust and the adverts confirm it: the 47 m² unit's two headline prices are one unit quoted two ways — the body states a co-op transfer of 3,399,000 and an **anuita** of 5,126,000, which sum to 8,525,000 exactly — and the 21-advert and 13-advert chains have **zero overlapping windows**, while the engine still rejects the block's genuinely distinct sreality twins (`16439 x 16442`). (ii) **The bar is met on a fresh seal and the incumbent fails part of it.** Sealed: 204 of 250 against 164, McNemar gained 40 lost 0 (p ~ 1.8e-12), precision 1.0 (Wilson95 lower 0.9815), **0 reliable false merges at pair, block, family and cluster grain in both arms** (M132). Dev + validation: merge 5,158 / band 4,524 / groups 980 against 4,690 / 4,907 / 939, reliable recall 0.8332 against 0.7140 (gained 140, lost 0, p ~ 1.4e-42), and **0/0/0/0 against g6's one cluster-grain false merge** `445691 x 15427866` (M127). The band SHRINKS, so the LLM bill falls with the promotion. The verifier's hazard read — 68 new merges by hand across 24 blocks and 23 K-B families, including all 21 non-K-B ones and a seeded random 40 — found **0 different-unit merges** (Wilson95 upper 0.0535 pair / 0.1380 block / 0.1431 family). (iii) **What is promoted, exactly**: `autodedup/settings/w13.json` with `autodedup/models/w6_gold.json` (no refit, no cut moved, no stratum changed — the arm moves the CLOCK), evidenced by `autodedup/settings/w13_strata.json`. Nothing under `settings/refuted/` is promoted and E87's convention keeps only its first half, as E110 records. (iv) **What this acceptance rests on, and what revokes it, unchanged from E110 and now COUNTED (E111)**: it rests on one operator ruling about one development, and an operator NEGATIVE inside a K-B family takes it back — a settings change, not a code change. Today the check reads 0 over 196 operator-labelled pairs in 173 families (M134). Two pairs are owed to the validation UI before anyone calls the question closed, and they are committed as `autodedup/pairs/w13_operator_owed.json`: `145827 x 11648789`, which the build and the verification independently read as ONE UNIT and which g7 MERGES as K-B, and `16438 x 92824`, which merges in BOTH arms and is therefore an incumbent question nobody has ruled. (v) **D30 (vii)'s residual risk is UNCHANGED and is not softened by this wave's clean read**: a developer posting N distinct units serially under one broker key at one price, one area and one gallery is still merged into one property, and this 5,687-listing cohort contains no detected instance. Five guards have now been tried against it and refused on their measurements (E65 floor, E83 carrier-aware stock, E85 family guard, E88 hold; E84 is sound and ships). (vi) **The real-time lane carries g7 with no new rail.** The honest clock is a clock and a pair-level rail, not a cohort-wide index: `incremental.run_pass` raises only on `catalog_carrier_aware`, `development_hold_mode != off` and `family_guard_mode != off`, and w13 sets all three to the accepted value. Replay equivalence is exact and order-insensitive on this row — 48,908 pairs and 15,923 stored pairs differing in 0, 980 clusters with identical member sets, unchanged under `--shuffle-seed 7`. The lane takes the row on its own dispatch (`settings=w13`) — there is no repo pin to flip — and the shadow schedule stays where the real-time wave left it: this decision changes the RULES the lane runs, not when it runs. (vii) **Two things the promotion note may not lose**: exactly one merge is lost against g6 — `531470 x 18717549`, unlabelled and reading as a true cross-portal duplicate (M133), so “nothing is lost” is a claim about LABELLED RELIABLE duplicates only; and 122 of the 342 pairs on the adjudicating test side were already test under W12's seed for this same arm (35.7 %, confirmation overlap, no search run there — M132). (viii) **W14 is owed the floor convention** (M135): sreality is 1-based where the other portals are 0-based, it costs 842 `floor_spread` cluster-edge refusals in g7, and it is the largest nameable residual recall cause. Evidence: `autodedup/settings/w13_strata.json`.| W13 verification + assembly, 2026-09-20 |

**D6 status, 2026-09-20 (W6).** D6's stop is **measured and FAILING**: gold agrees with the operator on **92.65 %** of decided pairs, Wilson 95 % [88.22, 95.49], against the 0.95 bar (M46, run `35469533084`). It is recorded, not waived, and it does not stop the program, because the thing D6 exists to protect against — a circular engine validated by its own judge — is now moot on the surface that matters: **the operator is the reference** (PR #1528), gold is demoted to a corroborating tier wherever operator labels exist, and every W6 promotion clause was measured against operator rulings rather than judge labels. The failure is itself a finding about gold, not about the engine: gold is effectively two correlated `gpt-5-mini` votes (its third vote abstained on 79 % of pairs), it makes 11 false merges of 42 negatives on these pairs, and only 10 of 223 aggregates were unanimous. Re-running D6 on a judge stack this wave did not fix would measure the same thing again.


**Extra scope rulings (same date, same authority).** (i) The SPA gets a **progress page in the top-line menu** showing each iteration — sample statistics, approach, tools used, cost — backed by `autodedup.iterations`, and it **ships in W1, before the validation views**. (ii) **W2's feature scope starts at a core set of ~25 and grows only by error analysis**; certificates K-A/K-B/K-C stay. (iii) **The only shared writes in this program are the three `llm_calls.called_for` values**; no other shared table is written.

## 16. Standing notes

- **Numbers are cited by code and tests — never renumber E1–E111.** A rule that changes meaning gets a new number and the old one is marked superseded.
- **Numbers are cited by code and tests — never renumber E1–E111.** A rule that changes meaning gets a new number and the old one is marked superseded.
- **A merge is not a deploy, and a merged PR is not an applied migration.** Migrations apply via `apply_migration.yml` *before* the PR merges; Railway rollout is confirmed via `gh api repos/{owner}/{repo}/commits/<sha>/status`.
- **Shadow is the mode.** If any document, comment or code path in this program implies a production write, it is wrong (D4).
- **A split seal lives in the repository, not in scratch.** `harness fit` writes the split map beside the model it fits, and the map is committed as `autodedup/splits/<sha256>.json`; `--split-map` takes a committed seal wherever it takes a path, and `evaluate` finds a model's own seal without a flag. W6 opened with the W4/W5 maps gone to a tmpfs wipe, so `w4_gold`, `w4f_gold` and `w5_gold` cannot be re-measured on the holdout they were fitted on: those two seals are recorded in `autodedup.seals.LOST_SEALS` with the reason, and `tests/autodedup/test_seals.py` refuses any shipped model that names a seal which is neither committed nor recorded.
- **No lane artifact carries the operator's identity.** `out/` is uploaded to a GitHub Actions artifact of a PUBLIC repository. `autodedup.verdicts.decided_by` is a login (an e-mail), so the `labels` lane ships it as a salted digest — E28's broker idiom — and `tests/autodedup/test_artifact_identity_census.py` holds the rail for every lane writer.
- **A 429 that follows a success is pacing, not an empty account** (D17's first operational finding, fixed 2026-09-20). DashScope answers a rate wall in OpenAI's billing wording, and W6 lost two Qwen arms to it — at six and at three workers, after 13 and 21 verdicts — while `workers=1` finished the same pass. `judge_lane.classify_provider_error` now takes `after_success`, passed only by the retry loop: a **429** on an arm that has already answered in this run is a rate limit (jittered 2/4/8 ladder, then a per-arm step-down — parallelism first, then spacing, at most `MAX_STEP_DOWNS`), and the identical wording on an arm that has never answered is still quota and still writes the arm off at once. Once the ladder and the step-downs are spent the error is re-classified WITHOUT the flag, so a real exhaustion still puts the arm (or the run) down, merely later. The secondary arm has its own dials — `qwen_workers` (default **1**) and `qwen_min_interval_ms` — because `workers` is the primary model's parallelism and it was never the one being throttled; `summary.json` carries `pacing` per arm (step-downs, backoff seconds, paced seconds) plus flat `pacing_step_downs` / `pacing_backoff_s`. D17's second finding — the gold artifact double-booking cost — is untouched and still open.
- **This document is the program's source of truth.** If a PR changes behaviour described here, it updates this file in the same PR.

---

## Appendix A — Beyond this program

**Nothing in this appendix is a deliverable of this program.** It exists so the rollout conversation starts from written numbers instead of a blank page. Each item needs its own operator decision, taken *after* W6's decision package.

**A1 — Canary on one block (the former W8).** `autodedup_write_enabled=true` for **one small block only**, one week, with a before/after diff of Browse, Stats, watchdog match counts and the pipeline board (E44/D5). Gate: the operator approves the diff, plus a weekly precision re-audit on 200 fresh merges. Estimated ~$5.

**A2 — Staged backfill of the corpus (the former W9; out of scope by D9).** Region cohorts, newest-first, monthly slices, the revert script proven on a real generation, each slice inside the $150/month envelope and haltable at any slice boundary. The arithmetic, for when that conversation happens: 822,000 × 20 / 2 = **8.2M distinct pairs** (a pair is scored once, not once per side). Free-signal scoring is DB + CPU — at a measured 56.5M pairs in 2h32m of DB time, well under an hour of DB plus a few hours of Actions CPU, **$0**. The LLM band at `b = 0.015` ⇒ 123,000 × $0.0017 = **$209**; at `b = 0.008` (achievable once the model is trained on backfill-era pairs, plus auto-rejecting low-prior pairs where both sides are long-inactive) ⇒ **$111**. **Outside the $200 development budget** either way.

**A3 — The shared-table index.** If the image lane's latency turns out to depend on an index over `images(phash)`, that is its own operator-confirmed migration under a **new name** — `images_phash_idx` already exists on `images(sreality_id)` and an `IF NOT EXISTS` would silently no-op. D8 keeps it out of this program entirely.

**A4 — Coexistence with the parallel operator-guided program.** Two engines writing through one merge chokepoint will eventually disagree. That is a rollout-time design problem, and under D7 it may **not** be solved by importing the operator's merge or unmerge history into this engine.

**A5 — The revert rehearsal (the former §9 metric 10).** Timing a 500-merge revert is the only way to turn §6's reversibility *argument* into a measurement, and it cannot be done in shadow: `merge_properties` writes `properties`, re-points `listings.property_id`, logs `property_merge_events` and carries operator state (`carry_operator_state_on_merge`, `reconcile_pipeline_on_merge`) inside one transaction, with `recompute_one` / `recompute_mf_one` / `sync_browse_list` inline per merge. A rehearsal therefore applies 500 real merges under one `generation` label, runs `scripts/autodedup_revert.py --generation <g>`, and diffs the browse and stats read models before and after. It needs **explicit operator sign-off, a `pg_dump` first, and a named blast radius** — one block, newest listings only. Pass: ≤ 30 min and consistent read models. Until it happens, every threshold argument in this program carries the caveat in risk 10.
